נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳©׳•׳¢׳¨ ׳‘׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳׳©׳¨׳“׳™׳ ׳׳׳•׳‘׳˜׳—. ׳”׳©׳•׳¢׳¨ ׳׳—׳–׳™׳§ ׳‘׳׳›׳©׳™׳¨ ׳׳׳§׳˜׳¨׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳§׳•׳¨׳ ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳’׳ ׳˜׳™׳™׳ ׳•׳₪׳•׳×׳— ׳“׳׳×׳•׳× ג€“ ׳–׳”׳• ׳”׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ (Mechanism), ׳•׳”׳•׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳¢׳•׳׳. ׳׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳–׳׳×, ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳©׳׳•׳¨׳©׳™׳ ׳׳”׳™׳›׳ ׳¡ ׳׳§׳•׳׳” 4 ׳׳—׳¨׳™ ׳”׳©׳¢׳” 20:00 ׳”׳™׳ ׳”׳׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳× (Policy). ׳׳ ׳ ׳¦׳˜׳¨׳ ׳׳”׳—׳׳™׳£ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ ׳”׳₪׳™׳–׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳©׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳׳×׳—׳׳£, ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳×׳§׳¨׳•׳¡. ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳“׳•׳׳”, ׳׳ ׳›׳ ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳× ׳×׳§׳‘׳ ׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳׳׳¡׳˜׳¨ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘ (׳›׳׳• ׳׳©׳×׳׳© root ׳‘׳׳×׳™ ׳׳•׳’׳‘׳), ׳›׳ ׳‘׳׳’ ׳–׳¢׳™׳¨ ׳‘׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳” ׳™׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳׳₪׳¨׳¦׳× ׳׳‘׳˜׳—׳” ׳§׳˜׳¡׳˜׳¨׳•׳₪׳׳™׳×.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳ ׳×׳— ׳‘׳‘׳™׳× ׳—׳•׳׳™׳. ׳”׳¨׳•׳₪׳ ׳–׳§׳•׳§ ׳׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳×׳™׳§ ׳”׳¨׳₪׳•׳׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳” ׳‘׳¢׳× ׳”׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳—, ׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳–׳§׳•׳§ ׳׳©׳•׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳×׳™׳§׳™ ׳”׳ ׳”׳׳× ׳”׳—׳©׳‘׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳‘׳™׳× ׳”׳—׳•׳׳™׳, ׳•׳’׳ ׳׳×׳™׳§ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳” ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳–׳§׳•׳§ ׳›׳©׳”׳•׳ ׳׳—׳•׳¥ ׳׳׳©׳׳¨׳×. ׳׳ ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳” ׳׳§׳‘׳׳× ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳§׳•׳‘׳¦׳™ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳׳©׳ ׳›׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳¨׳™׳¦׳×׳”, ׳›׳ ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳¦׳“-׳©׳׳™׳©׳™ ׳©׳ ׳˜׳¢׳ ׳׳×׳•׳›׳” ׳™׳•׳›׳ ׳׳’׳ ׳•׳‘ ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳¨׳’׳™׳©.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳§׳‘׳׳” ׳‘׳‘׳™׳× ׳׳׳•׳ ׳”׳׳—׳–׳™׳§ ׳‘׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳׳׳¡׳˜׳¨ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳. ׳׳’׳™׳¢ ׳׳•׳¨׳— ׳׳—׳“׳¨ 101 ׳•׳׳•׳׳¨: "׳×׳ ׳¢׳ ׳‘׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳׳× ׳—׳“׳¨ 202 ׳‘׳©׳‘׳™׳׳™". ׳”׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳”׳׳׳¡׳˜׳¨ ׳©׳׳• ׳•׳ ׳•׳¢׳ ׳׳× ׳—׳“׳¨ 202, ׳›׳™ ׳׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳¢׳¦׳׳• ׳™׳© ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳, ׳׳‘׳׳™ ׳׳•׳•׳“׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳׳׳•׳¨׳— ׳׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳₪׳¢׳•׳ ׳¢׳ ׳—׳“׳¨ 202! ׳”׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳›"׳¡׳’׳ ׳׳‘׳•׳׳‘׳" ׳©׳ ׳•׳¦׳ ׳׳¨׳¢׳”.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳©׳¢׳¨ ׳—׳©׳׳׳™ ׳©׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳–׳™׳”׳•׳™ ׳©׳‘׳‘. ׳׳ ׳”׳׳“׳ ׳”׳ ׳•׳©׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳‘׳‘ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳׳•׳¨׳©׳”, ׳”׳©׳¢׳¨ ׳׳—׳•׳©׳׳ ׳•׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳™׳“ ׳¡׳™׳¨׳ ׳”. ׳‘׳׳¢׳‘׳“, ׳©׳¢׳¨ ׳—׳©׳׳׳™ ׳–׳” ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳¢׳’׳ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳©׳ ׳”-MMU: ׳‘׳›׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳ ׳‘׳“׳§׳× ׳¨׳׳× ׳”׳”׳¨׳©׳׳” ׳”׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳•׳ ׳¡׳™׳‘׳™׳× ׳”׳”׳¨׳©׳׳” ׳‘׳¨׳׳× ׳”׳“׳£. ׳׳ ׳™׳© ׳׳™-׳”׳×׳׳׳”, ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳—׳•׳¡׳׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳–׳¨׳ ׳•׳׳–׳¢׳™׳§׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳”.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

  • ׳¡׳™׳‘׳™׳× U/S ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳× ׳”׳“׳₪׳™׳: ׳”-MMU ׳‘׳•׳“׳§ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳: ׳׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳‘-Ring 3 ׳•׳”׳“׳£ ׳׳¡׳•׳׳ ׳›-$U/S = 0$ (Supervisor), ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨ ׳׳™׳“ ׳₪׳¡׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳’׳ ׳”: Page Fault (#PF).
  • ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳׳™׳•׳—׳¡׳•׳×: ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳¨׳’׳™׳¡׳˜׳¨ ׳‘׳§׳¨׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ (CR3), ׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳“׳’׳׳™ ׳₪׳¡׳™׳§׳•׳× (CLI/STI), ׳׳• ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜׳™ I/O ׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨׳•׳× General Protection Fault (#GP) ׳׳ ׳׳•׳₪׳¢׳׳•׳× ׳‘-Ring 3.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳¦׳•׳׳׳×. ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳׳× ׳׳•׳ ׳•׳׳™׳×׳™׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳׳—׳™׳¦׳•׳× ׳׳˜׳•׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳ ג€“ ׳”׳›׳ ׳₪׳×׳•׳— ׳•׳׳¨׳•׳•׳—, ׳•׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳”׳™׳¨ ׳׳׳•׳“ (׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳’׳‘׳•׳”׳™׳). ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳™׳© ׳—׳•׳¨ ׳§׳˜׳ ׳‘׳׳—׳“ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳, ׳›׳ ׳”׳¦׳•׳׳׳× ׳˜׳•׳‘׳¢׳×. ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳׳× ׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳¨׳™׳× (׳׳™׳§׳¨׳•-׳§׳¨׳ ׳) ׳™׳© ׳“׳׳×׳•׳× ׳₪׳׳“׳” ׳׳˜׳•׳׳•׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳›׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳. ׳׳ ׳—׳“׳¨ ׳׳—׳“ ׳׳•׳¦׳£, ׳¡׳•׳’׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳“׳׳× ׳•׳”׳¦׳•׳׳׳× ׳׳׳©׳™׳›׳” ׳׳©׳•׳˜, ׳׳׳¨׳•׳× ׳©׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳“׳•׳¨׳© ׳₪׳×׳™׳—׳” ׳•׳¡׳’׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳™׳˜׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳“׳׳×׳•׳×.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳‘׳©׳™׳•׳ ׳¡׳˜׳˜׳™, ׳—׳™׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳¦׳ ׳׳§׳¨׳‘ ׳¢׳ ׳›׳ ׳”׳¦׳™׳•׳“ ׳”׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳™ ג€“ ׳¨׳•׳‘׳”, ׳₪׳˜׳™׳© 5 ׳§"׳’, ׳׳›׳©׳™׳¨ ׳§׳©׳¨ ׳›׳‘׳“ ׳•׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳”׳ ׳©׳§ ג€“ ׳•׳”׳•׳ ׳—׳™׳™׳‘ ׳׳¡׳—׳•׳‘ ׳”׳›׳ ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳›׳ ׳”׳׳¡׳¢. ׳‘׳׳•׳“׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™, ׳”׳—׳™׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳¦׳ ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳•׳‘׳” ׳‘׳׳‘׳“; ׳›׳©׳”׳•׳ ׳׳’׳™׳¢ ׳׳“׳׳× ׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳”, ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳₪׳×׳—, ׳₪׳•׳×׳—, ׳•׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳™׳“ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳₪׳×׳—. ׳”׳׳•׳“׳ ׳”׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™ ׳“׳•׳¨׳© ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳׳•׳’׳™׳¡׳˜׳™ (׳×׳§׳•׳¨׳”), ׳׳ ׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳׳•׳‘׳“׳ ׳©׳ ׳׳₪׳×׳—׳•׳× ׳¨׳’׳™׳©׳™׳.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

  • ׳©׳™׳•׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™ (Domain Switching): ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳׳•׳¨׳©׳” ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳׳×׳—׳•׳ $D_1$ ׳׳×׳—׳•׳ $D_2$. ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳–׳” ׳׳˜׳•׳₪׳ ׳›׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳™׳•׳—׳“׳× ׳׳• ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳©׳¢׳¨ (Call Gate), ׳©׳‘׳• ׳ ׳‘׳“׳§׳× ׳”׳–׳›׳•׳× ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ Switch ׳׳×׳—׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳¢׳“.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳×׳—׳•׳׳™׳ ׳׳׳ ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳” ׳•׳©׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳ ׳§׳™׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳¨׳’׳™׳¡׳˜׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳¢׳׳•׳ ׳׳“׳׳•׳£ ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳¨׳’׳™׳© ׳׳”׳×׳—׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳•׳—׳¡ ׳׳×׳—׳•׳ ׳”׳‘׳׳×׳™-׳׳™׳•׳—׳¡ (Side-Channel Information Leakage).

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳׳–׳¨׳— ׳©׳¨׳•׳¦׳” ׳׳©׳ ׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳× ׳©׳׳• ׳‘׳׳©׳¨׳“ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳. ׳”׳׳–׳¨׳— ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳¨׳©׳׳™ ׳׳”׳™׳›׳ ׳¡ ׳׳׳׳’׳¨ ׳”׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳”׳׳׳©׳׳×׳™ ׳•׳׳¢׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳×. ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳–׳׳×, ׳”׳•׳ ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳©׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳” ׳׳™׳•׳—׳“׳× ׳׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳×׳¢׳•׳“׳× ׳–׳”׳•׳× ׳•׳¢׳“׳›׳•׳. ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ SetUID ׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳‘׳“׳™׳•׳§ ׳›׳: ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳× (׳›׳׳• passwd) ׳©׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳” ׳׳§׳‘׳׳× ׳–׳׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳× ׳¡׳׳›׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ (׳׳©׳×׳׳© root) ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳¢׳“׳›׳ ׳׳× ׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳”׳¡׳™׳¡׳׳׳•׳× ׳”׳׳•׳’׳, ׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳•׳×׳•!

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳”-SUID: ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” ׳‘׳¢׳ ׳¡׳™׳‘׳™׳× SetUID ׳“׳•׳׳§׳× ׳׳•׳₪׳¢׳ ׳¢׳ ׳™׳“׳™ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× execve(), ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳’׳“׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳× ׳”-Effective UID (eUID) ׳©׳ ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳׳–׳”׳•׳×׳• ׳©׳ ׳‘׳¢׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ (File Owner, ׳׳¨׳•׳‘ root), ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳-Real UID (rUID) ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳©׳”׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳׳•׳×׳•.

  • ׳”׳¡׳›׳ ׳” ׳”׳׳‘׳˜׳—׳×׳™׳×: ׳׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳× ׳‘׳¢׳׳× ׳”-SUID ׳׳›׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳׳’ ׳₪׳©׳•׳˜ (׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳₪׳’׳™׳¢׳•׳× ׳’׳׳™׳©׳× ׳—׳•׳¦׳¥ ׳׳• ׳”׳–׳¨׳§׳× ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳“׳¨׳ ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™ ׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘׳” ׳›׳׳• LD_PRELOAD ׳׳• PATH), ׳”׳×׳•׳§׳£ ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳”׳–׳¨׳™׳§ ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× Shell ׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳¦׳• ׳‘׳”׳¨׳©׳׳•׳× root ׳׳׳׳•׳×!

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳•׳× SetUID ׳©׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳©׳׳•׳× ׳”-root (Privilege Dropping) ׳׳™׳“ ׳׳׳—׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢ ׳”׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳”׳׳™׳•׳—׳¡׳×, ׳׳• ׳©׳₪׳•׳×׳—׳•׳× ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™ ׳‘׳ (fork/exec) ׳‘׳¢׳•׳“׳ ׳׳—׳–׳™׳§׳•׳× ׳‘-eUID=0.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳’׳™׳׳™׳•׳ ׳׳׳§׳˜׳¨׳•׳ ׳™ (Excel) ׳©׳‘׳• ׳™׳© ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳׳–׳¨׳— ׳‘׳׳“׳™׳ ׳” (10 ׳׳™׳׳™׳•׳ ׳©׳•׳¨׳•׳×) ׳•׳¢׳׳•׳“׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳•׳׳¡׳׳ ׳‘׳׳“׳™׳ ׳” (׳׳™׳׳™׳׳¨׳“ ׳¢׳׳•׳“׳•׳×). ׳¨׳•׳‘ ׳¨׳•׳‘׳ ׳”׳׳•׳—׳׳˜ ׳©׳ ׳”׳×׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳˜׳‘׳׳” ׳”׳–׳• ׳™׳”׳™׳• ׳¨׳™׳§׳™׳ ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳, ׳׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳©׳›׳ ׳׳–׳¨׳— ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳¨׳§ ׳׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳§׳˜׳ ׳©׳ ׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳ ׳׳™׳©׳™׳™׳. ׳©׳׳™׳¨׳× ׳”׳˜׳‘׳׳” ׳”׳–׳• ׳‘׳©׳׳׳•׳×׳” ׳×׳“׳¨׳•׳© ׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘׳™׳ ׳‘׳¢׳•׳׳ ׳‘׳™׳—׳“! ׳׳›׳, ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳•׳¨ ׳˜׳‘׳׳” ׳¨׳™׳§׳” ׳¢׳ ׳§׳™׳×, ׳׳“׳‘׳™׳§׳™׳ ׳׳›׳ ׳׳¡׳׳ ׳₪׳×׳§ ׳§׳˜׳ (ACL) ׳•׳¢׳׳™׳• ׳¨׳©׳•׳׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©׳™׳ ׳”׳‘׳•׳“׳“׳™׳ ׳©׳׳•׳¨׳©׳™׳ ׳׳’׳©׳× ׳׳׳™׳•.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

  • ׳©׳™׳˜׳” 3: ׳”׳’׳ ׳” ׳§׳¨׳™׳₪׳˜׳•׳’׳¨׳₪׳™׳× (Cryptographic Capabilities): ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳׳‘׳•׳–׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳• ׳׳׳ ׳×׳׳™׳›׳× ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳”, ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳—׳©׳‘׳× ׳—׳×׳™׳׳” ׳׳•׳¦׳₪׳ ׳×: $\text{Signature} = \text{HMAC}_{K_{\text{secret}}}(\text{ObjectID} \parallel \text{Rights} \parallel \text{RandomSalt})$. ׳”׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡ ׳ ׳׳¡׳¨ ׳׳׳©׳×׳׳©. ׳‘׳¢׳× ׳”׳¦׳’׳× ׳”׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡, ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳—׳©׳‘׳× ׳׳—׳“׳© ׳׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳‘׳•׳‘; ׳׳ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳©׳™׳ ׳” ׳‘׳™׳˜ ׳‘׳•׳“׳“ (׳׳׳©׳ ׳-Read ׳-Write), ׳”׳—׳×׳™׳׳” ׳׳ ׳×׳×׳׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳×׳™׳“׳—׳” ׳›׳–׳™׳•׳£!

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳ ׳׳ ׳×׳¢׳•׳₪׳”. ׳‘׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳˜׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳“ ׳©׳•׳˜׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳ ׳•׳¡׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳•׳¨׳©׳™׳ (ACL) ג€“ ׳׳×׳׳™׳ ׳׳׳•׳“ ׳׳©׳׳™׳˜׳” ׳§׳‘׳•׳¢׳” ׳•׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳” ׳¢׳ ׳׳™ ׳¨׳©׳׳™ ׳׳”׳™׳›׳ ׳¡. ׳׳ ׳‘׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳©׳׳™׳©׳¨׳• ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳×׳›׳, ׳׳ ׳₪׳™׳§׳™׳ ׳׳›׳ ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡ ׳¢׳׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳˜׳•׳¡ (Boarding Pass - Capability). ׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳˜׳•׳¡, ׳”׳“׳™׳™׳׳× ׳›׳‘׳¨ ׳׳ ׳׳—׳₪׳©׳× ׳׳× ׳©׳׳›׳ ׳‘׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™ ׳”׳ ׳׳, ׳׳׳ ׳¡׳•׳¨׳§׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳‘׳¨׳§׳•׳“ ׳©׳‘׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡ ׳©׳׳›׳ ׳‘׳©׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳”. ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳ ׳”׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳©׳ ׳™ ׳”׳¢׳•׳׳׳•׳×: ׳‘׳§׳¨׳× ׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳ ׳™׳¦׳™׳‘׳” ׳“׳¨׳ ACL, ׳•׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™ ׳˜׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳”׳™׳¨׳™׳ ׳“׳¨׳ ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡׳™ ׳¢׳׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳˜׳•׳¡!
